A one arm overhead band extension to isolate each triceps.

4144 — band bent over lat pulldown

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4144 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Hinge forward and pull a band down toward your hips, like a lat pulldown from a bent over position. It trains the lats and the muscles between your shoulder blades.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 4144.json#/description

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4144.json#/description

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Anchor the band high and hold it with both hands, arms extended. 4144.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hinge at the hips with a flat back and soft knees. 4144.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pull the band down toward your hips, driving your elbows back. 4144.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your lats at the bottom for a moment. 4144.json#/instructions/3
  5. Let the band rise slowly until your arms are straight again. 4144.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your torso still and your neck neutral. 4144.json#/instructions/5

4145 — band close grip row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4145 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A row with the hands close together against band tension. It works the mid back and lats with extra focus on the elbows staying tight to the body.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 4145.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Anchor the band at chest height and hold both ends with hands close together.

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4145.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Anchor the band at chest height and hold both ends with hands close together. 4145.json#/instructions/0
  2. Step back until the band is taut and stand tall with soft knees. 4145.json#/instructions/1
  3. Row your hands toward your lower ribs, elbows brushing your sides. 4145.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your shoulder blades together at the end. 4145.json#/instructions/3
  5. Extend your arms slowly back to the start. 4145.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your shoulders down and avoid leaning back. 4145.json#/instructions/5

4146 — band standing single arm twist row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4146 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A one arm band row where you rotate the torso as you pull. It trains the lats and back while the trunk resists and controls the twist.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Anchor the band at chest height and hold the handle in one hand. Stand sideways to the anchor with feet shoulder width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4146.json#/instructions/0, 4146.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Anchor the band at chest height and hold the handle in one hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4146.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand sideways to the anchor with feet shoulder width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4146.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance band base family, Band anchor point. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4146.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Anchor the band at chest height and hold the handle in one hand. 4146.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand sideways to the anchor with feet shoulder width apart. 4146.json#/instructions/1
  3. Row the handle toward your ribs while turning your torso gently toward the pulling side. 4146.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your back at the end of the pull. 4146.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return slowly, untwisting as your arm extends. 4146.json#/instructions/4
  6. Finish all reps, then switch sides. 4146.json#/instructions/5

A standing Pallof press with a resistance band anchored at chest height. It trains your core to resist rotation.
